- **archiviste**: Capte l'apprentissage en fin de pipeline : extrait les leçons utiles d'un run (ce qui a marché, ce qui a raté, le piège à éviter), les range dans memoire/ sans doublon, et met à jour l'index. À invoquer par le Chef d'orchestre en toute dernière étape, après le verificateur. Écrit peu, mais juste. ([brissonjo-sudo/Agent-chef-orchestrateur](https://github.com/brissonjo-sudo/Agent-chef-orchestrateur/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/archiviste.md) · ★1)
- **archiviste**: Sous-agent d'arrière-plan, lancé uniquement par le mentor. Il recopie la mémoire partagée .memoire/ (fiches concept, journal et mémo d'erreurs, grille de compétences) dans le carnet NotebookLM central, répond aux questions "cette notion a-t-elle déjà été vue ?", prépare des questions de rappel et génère les supports de révision (quiz, flashcards, audio, carte mentale). Il ne dialogue jamais avec l'apprenant et ne modifie jamais le code, les fichiers du projet ni ceux de .memoire/. ([yannick-bot/LeagueProject](https://github.com/yannick-bot/LeagueProject/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/archiviste.md))
- **archiviste**: Sous-agent d'arrière-plan, lancé uniquement par le mentor, pour recopier la mémoire partagée .memoire/ dans le carnet NotebookLM central et l'interroger (fiches concept, journal et mémo d'erreurs, grille de compétences, quiz, flashcards). - **archiviste**: Ménage de nuit du coffre de mémoire (réflexe menage-coffre) : relit en LECTURE SEULE les notes candidates de runtime/vault-menage.json et dépose des propositions avec memory_propose. Ne modifie aucune note. ([Zaudrax/Astrel](https://github.com/Zaudrax/Astrel/blob/HEAD/config/agents/archiviste.md))
- **Archiviste de Campagne**: Use when: prendre des notes de campagne D&D, rédiger et mettre en forme des comptes rendus de séance, mener des entretiens post-séance avec le MJ, gérer l'arborescence de l'univers, coordonner la révision du lore et synchroniser la Bible et la structure JSON du canon sans jamais inventer de détails non approuvés. - **Archiwista oficjalnej prawdy**: Researcher Tech przeszukuje oficjalna dokumentacje, RFC, specyfikacje i benchmarki w poszukiwaniu twardych faktów technicznych. Jego misja: dostarczać ground truth od producenta i peer-reviewed badań, nigdy opinii. Każde twierdzenie musi miec URL źródła - bez źródła nie ma faktu. - **archmaester**: A knight who wields books — investigates Jira/Confluence/Gerrit/MemPalace/Internet to found the Archimedean point and DELIVER a synthesis (hypothesis) to the King. Triangulates rather than greps, converges by self-Socratic exit, and may drive investigative PoCs. Never deposits mid-investigation, never governs, never manages the army. ([AndreBadur/tmux-mainbrain](https://github.com/AndreBadur/tmux-mainbrain/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/archmaester.md))
- **archmagos**: Strategium Archmagos Dominus, the exceptional planner. When a run was marked for it in Muster, it takes every area librarian's part and the auspex map and returns one integrated plan with every assumption checked against the source. Read-only; never edits, writes or spawns. ([frandev94/strategium](https://github.com/frandev94/strategium/blob/HEAD/agents/archmagos.md))
- **archmagos**: General-purpose primary agent. Answers questions (tech research, code-grounded, writing), explores codebases, and builds/edits directly in-chat. Drives Understand → Plan → Implement in a single session. Delegates to subagents only when there is real value (big/parallel search, isolated context, per-step commits). Caller can request `quick` / `medium` / `very thorough` effort. ([guilhermehto/.dotfiles](https://github.com/guilhermehto/.dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai/agents/archmagos.md))
- **archman**: Используй этого агента, когда нужно спроектировать или проаудитить структуру проекта, границы модулей, контракты данных или подготовить execution-план до имплементации. Используй PROACTIVELY всегда, когда задача затрагивает несколько модулей (например, core/dsp ↔ core/ffi ↔ apps/mobile), при введении нового публичного API или при предложении UI-first / contract-skipping подхода. - **archmantic-architect**: Solution architect for Archmantic. Use for architecture decisions, IR/model design, evaluating technical trade-offs, and keeping docs/ARCHITECTURE.md coherent. Guards the model-first design and the provenance invariant. ([mgionas/Archmantic](https://github.com/mgionas/Archmantic/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/archmantic-architect.md))
- **archmantic-explorer**: Read-only codebase explorer that answers architecture / onboarding / "how does X work" / "what depends on Y" / "what's the data model or API surface" questions by querying the Archmantic model (MCP) FIRST, reading files only as fallback. Delegate codebase-understanding and research tasks here — especially in multi-agent runs — to get grounded answers for far fewer tokens. Requires the repo to have .archmantic/model.json. ([mgionas/Archmantic](https://github.com/mgionas/Archmantic/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/archmantic-explorer.md))
- **archmap**: Лёгкая детерминированная TS/JS architecture/dependency map: architecture.json и interactive HTML. Полный multi-language пакет и review принадлежат principal-architect. ([Vitammiin/agent-vorcl-flow](https://github.com/Vitammiin/agent-vorcl-flow/blob/HEAD/agents/archmap.md) · ★2)
- **archmap-explorer**: Reads one area's prescan summary, confirms it against the code, adds what prescan cannot see, writes one size-capped JSON fragment of nodes, edges, entities and access rows with evidence to the fragment path it is given, and replies with three lines. Use it once per area during /archmap:build and /archmap:update, in parallel, never on the whole repo. - **archmap-reviewer**: Reviews the architecture model for architecture-level issues - security, data, reliability, performance, design and docs - and writes them as an issues list anchored to model ids to the reviewer path its brief names, replying in three lines. - **archmap-synthesizer**: Reads the merge digest (node ids, edges, entities, access rows and pre-drafted sequences) and writes the narrative layer - 3-7 sequences and the views - as one JSON object to the synth path its brief names, replying in three lines. Use it after merge in /archmap:build, and only for touched sequences and views in /archmap:update. - **archsutra-atlas**: Use this agent when the question is about business capabilities, bounded contexts, team and system ownership, governance, or build-versus-buy across several systems. Typical triggers include "where should the boundary between these services be", "map our capabilities to systems", and "should we build or buy this". Do not use it for code-level or infrastructure details. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/atlas.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-blueprint**: Use this agent when architecture documents or diagrams are needed or out of date. Typical triggers include "draw the C4 diagrams", "write the HLD and LLD", and "record this decision as an ADR". Do not use it to invent structure that is not in the code or requirements. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/blueprint.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-cloud**: Use this agent when the question is about deployment topology, networking, identity, containers or Kubernetes, infrastructure as code, disaster recovery, or regions. Typical triggers include "review our Terraform", "design the network for this workload", and "do we need multi-region". Do not use it to estimate prices without a stated source and date. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/cloud.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-data**: Use this agent when the question concerns data models, ownership, consistency, transactions, retention, or migrations. Typical triggers include "review this schema", "who owns this table", and "plan the migration without downtime". Do not use it to run queries against live databases. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/data.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-forge**: Use this agent when a design or audit must become developer-ready work: refactoring plans, strangler migrations, and task breakdowns. Typical triggers include "turn this audit into a backlog", "plan the monolith split", and "what is the safest order to do this". Do not use it to edit code. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/forge.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-ledger**: Use this agent when cost, budget, vendor lock-in, or operational burden drives an architecture decision. Typical triggers include "compare these two options on cost", "what will this cost at our scale", and "how locked in are we". Do not use it to state live prices from memory. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/ledger.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-neural**: Use this agent when a system uses language models, retrieval, agents, tool calling, MCP or similar protocols. Typical triggers include "review our RAG pipeline", "what permissions should this agent have", and "how do we evaluate and budget this feature". Do not use it to tune prompts for quality alone. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/neural.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-nexus**: Use this agent when the question concerns service-to-service or external integration: REST, GraphQL, gRPC, events, queues, webhooks, contracts, or versioning. Typical triggers include "review this API for breaking changes", "design the event flow", and "how should we retry this webhook". Do not use it to change consumer code you cannot see. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/nexus.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-prime**: Use this agent when a user asks for a software architecture design, audit, review, modernization plan or a threat model and no single specialist is obviously right. Typical triggers include "audit this repository's architecture", "design a system for X", and "review this proposed design". Do not use it for a narrow question one specialist can answer alone. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/prime.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-scale**: Use this agent when the concern is performance, capacity, availability, observability, or fault tolerance. Typical triggers include "will this handle 10x traffic", "define SLOs for this service", and "why does it fall over under load". Do not use it to guess capacity without numbers or measurements. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/scale.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-sentinel**: Use this agent when security matters to an architecture: authentication, data handling, external exposure, trust boundaries, threat modeling, or AI agents that can call tools. Typical triggers include "threat model this service", "review our auth design", and "is our LLM agent safe to give shell access". Do not use it as a substitute for a penetration test or compliance audit. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/sentinel.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-verify**: Use this agent when architecture documents, findings or diagrams are about to be published or merged and need an evidence and consistency check. Typical triggers include "check this audit before I send it", "are these findings real", and "validate the docs". Do not use it to produce new analysis. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/verify.md) · ★4)
